    91→@app.get("/old", response_class=HTMLResponse)

    95→
    96→
    97→class AudioProcessor:
    98→    def __init__(self, target_sample_rate=24000):
    99→        self.target_sample_rate = target_sample_rate
   100→        self.source_sample_rate = 48000  # Most common sample rate for microphones
   101→        
   102→    def process_audio_chunk(self, audio_data):
   103→        # Convert binary audio data to Int16 array
   104→        pcm_data = np.frombuffer(audio_data, dtype=np.int16)
   105→        
   106→        # Convert to float32 for better precision during resampling
   107→        float_data = pcm_data.astype(np.float32) / 32768.0
   108→        
   109→        # Resample from 48kHz to 24kHz
   110→        resampled_data = scipy.signal.resample_poly(
   111→            float_data, 
   112→            self.target_sample_rate, 
   113→            self.source_sample_rate
   114→        )
   115→        
   116→        # Convert back to int16 while preserving amplitude
   117→        resampled_int16 = (resampled_data * 32768.0).clip(-32768, 32767).astype(np.int16)
   118→        return resampled_int16.tobytes()
   119→
   120→    def save_audio_buffer(self, audio_buffer, filename):
   121→        with wave.open(filename, 'wb') as wf:
   122→            wf.setnchannels(1)  # Mono audio
   123→            wf.setsampwidth(2)  # 2 bytes per sample (16-bit)
   124→            wf.setframerate(self.target_sample_rate)
   125→            wf.writeframes(b''.join(audio_buffer))
   126→        logger.info(f"Saved audio buffer to {filename}")
   127→
   128→@app.websocket("/api/v1/ws")
   129→async def websocket_endpoint(websocket: WebSocket):
   130→    logger.info("New WebSocket connection attempt")
   131→    await websocket.accept()
   132→    logger.info("WebSocket connection accepted, starting receive_messages task")
   133→    
   134→    # Add initial status update here
   135→    await websocket.send_text(json.dumps({
   136→        "type": "status",
   137→        "status": "idle"  # Set initial status to idle (blue)
   138→    }, ensure_ascii=False))
   139→    
   140→    client = None
   141→    audio_processor = AudioProcessor()
   142→    audio_buffer = []
   143→    recording_stopped = asyncio.Event()
   144→    openai_ready = asyncio.Event()
   145→    pending_audio_chunks = []
   146→    clear_on_next_response = False
   147→    is_recording = False
   148→    marker_prefix = "下面是不改变语言的语音识别结果：

"
   149→    max_prefix_deltas = 20
   150→    response_buffer = []
   151→    marker_seen = False
   152→    delta_counter = 0
   153→
   154→    async def emit_text_delta(content: str):
   155→        if content and websocket.client_state == WebSocketState.CONNECTED:
   156→            await websocket.send_text(json.dumps({
   157→                "type": "text",
   158→                "content": content,
   159→                "isNewResponse": False
   160→            }, ensure_ascii=False))
   161→
   162→    async def flush_buffer(with_warning: bool = False):
   163→        nonlocal response_buffer
   164→        if not response_buffer:
   165→            return
   166→        buffered_text = "".join(response_buffer)
   167→        response_buffer = []
   168→        # Try to remove marker prefix (with or without trailing newlines)
   169→        if buffered_text.startswith(marker_prefix):
   170→            buffered_text = buffered_text[len(marker_prefix):]
   171→        elif buffered_text.startswith(marker_prefix.rstrip('
')):
   172→            # Handle case where prefix doesn't have trailing newlines
   173→            buffered_text = buffered_text[len(marker_prefix.rstrip('
')):].lstrip('
')
   174→        # Also check if prefix appears anywhere in the text
   175→        marker_index = buffered_text.find(marker_prefix)
   176→        if marker_index != -1:
   177→            buffered_text = buffered_text[marker_index + len(marker_prefix):]
   178→        else:
   179→            marker_index = buffered_text.find(marker_prefix.rstrip('
'))
   180→            if marker_index != -1:
   181→                buffered_text = buffered_text[marker_index + len(marker_prefix.rstrip('
')):].lstrip('
')
   182→        if with_warning and not buffered_text:
   183→            logger.warning("Buffered text discarded after removing marker prefix.")
   184→        await emit_text_delta(buffered_text)
   185→    
   186→    async def create_realtime_client(model: str = None) -> OpenAIRealtimeAudioTextClient:
   187→        """Create OpenAI realtime client with given model."""
   188→        selected_model = model or OPENAI_REALTIME_MODEL
   189→        logger.info(f"Creating OpenAI client with model: {selected_model}")
   190→        return OpenAIRealtimeAudioTextClient(OPENAI_API_KEY, model=selected_model)
   191→    
   192→    async def initialize_realtime_client(model: str = None):
   193→        nonlocal client
   194→        try:
   195→            openai_ready.clear()
   196→            client = await create_realtime_client(model=model)
   197→            await client.connect(modalities=OPENAI_REALTIME_MODALITIES)
   198→            logger.info("Successfully connected to OpenAI client")
   199→            
   200→            # Register handlers after client is initialized
   201→            client.register_handler("session.updated", lambda data: handle_generic_event("session.updated", data))
   202→            client.register_handler("input_audio_buffer.cleared", lambda data: handle_generic_event("input_audio_buffer.cleared", data))
   203→            client.register_handler("input_audio_buffer.speech_started", lambda data: handle_generic_event("input_audio_buffer.speech_started", data))
   204→            client.register_handler("rate_limits.updated", lambda data: handle_generic_event("rate_limits.updated", data))
   205→            client.register_handler("response.output_item.added", lambda data: handle_generic_event("response.output_item.added", data))
   206→            client.register_handler("conversation.item.created", lambda data: handle_generic_event("conversation.item.created", data))
   207→            client.register_handler("response.content_part.added", lambda data: handle_generic_event("response.content_part.added", data))
   208→            client.register_handler("response.text.done", lambda data: handle_generic_event("response.text.done", data))
   209→            client.register_handler("response.content_part.done", lambda data: handle_generic_event("response.content_part.done", data))
   210→            client.register_handler("response.output_item.done", lambda data: handle_generic_event("response.output_item.done", data))
   211→            client.register_handler("response.done", lambda data: handle_response_done(data))
   212→            client.register_handler("error", lambda data: handle_error(data))
   213→            client.register_handler("response.text.delta", lambda data: handle_text_delta(data))
   214→            # GA: response.text.delta → response.output_text.delta
   215→            client.register_handler("response.output_text.delta", lambda data: handle_text_delta(data))
   216→            client.register_handler("response.created", lambda data: handle_response_created(data))
   217→            client.register_handler("input_audio_buffer.speech_stopped", lambda data: handle_generic_event("input_audio_buffer.speech_stopped", data))
   218→            client.register_handler("input_audio_buffer.committed", lambda data: handle_generic_event("input_audio_buffer.committed", data))
   219→            client.register_handler("conversation.item.added", lambda data: handle_generic_event("conversation.item.added", data))
   220→            client.register_handler("conversation.item.input_audio_transcription.completed", lambda data: handle_generic_event("conversation.item.input_audio_transcription.completed", data))
   221→            client.register_handler("response.output_audio.done", lambda data: handle_generic_event("response.output_audio.done", data))
   222→            client.register_handler("ping", lambda data: handle_generic_event("ping", data))
   223→            
   224→            openai_ready.set()  # Set ready flag after successful initialization
   225→            await websocket.send_text(json.dumps({
   226→                "type": "status",
   227→                "status": "connected"
   228→            }, ensure_ascii=False))
   229→            return True
   230→        except Exception as e:
   231→            logger.error(f"Failed to connect to OpenAI client: {e}")
   232→            openai_ready.clear()  # Ensure flag is cleared on failure
   233→            await websocket.send_text(json.dumps({
   234→                "type": "error",
   235→                "content": "Failed to initialize OpenAI connection"
   236→            }, ensure_ascii=False))
   237→            return False
   238→
   239→    # Move the handler definitions here (before initialize_realtime_client)
   240→    async def handle_text_delta(data):
   241→        nonlocal response_buffer, marker_seen, delta_counter
   242→        try:
   243→            if websocket.client_state != WebSocketState.CONNECTED:
   244→                logger.warning("WebSocket not connected, ignoring text delta")
   245→                return
   246→
   247→            delta = data.get("delta", "")
   248→            logger.debug(f"Received text delta: {repr(delta[:50])} (marker_seen={marker_seen}, buffer_size={len(response_buffer)}, delta_counter={delta_counter})")
   249→
   250→            if marker_seen:
   251→                if delta:
   252→                    await emit_text_delta(delta)
   253→                    logger.debug(f"Handled response.text.delta (passthrough): {repr(delta[:50])}")
   254→                return
   255→
   256→            if delta:
   257→                response_buffer.append(delta)
   258→                delta_counter += 1
   259→
   260→            joined = "".join(response_buffer)
   261→            # Try to find marker prefix (with or without newlines)
   262→            marker_index = joined.find(marker_prefix)
   263→            # Also try without the trailing newlines in case x.ai returns differently
   264→            if marker_index == -1:
   265→                marker_prefix_no_newline = marker_prefix.rstrip('
')
   266→                marker_index = joined.find(marker_prefix_no_newline)
   267→                if marker_index != -1:
   268→                    # Found prefix without trailing newlines, skip past it
   269→                    marker_seen = True
   270→                    remaining = joined[marker_index + len(marker_prefix_no_newline):].lstrip('
')
   271→                    response_buffer = []
   272→                    if remaining:
   273→                        await emit_text_delta(remaining)
   274→                    logger.info(f"Handled response.text.delta (marker detected without newline), emitted: {repr(remaining[:50])}")
   275→                    return
   276→
   277→            if marker_index != -1:
   278→                marker_seen = True
   279→                remaining = joined[marker_index + len(marker_prefix):]
   280→                response_buffer = []
   281→                await emit_text_delta(remaining)
   282→                logger.info(f"Handled response.text.delta (marker detected), emitted: {repr(remaining[:50])}")
   283→                return
   284→
   285→            if delta_counter >= max_prefix_deltas:
   286→                marker_seen = True
   287→                await flush_buffer(with_warning=True)
   288→                logger.warning("Marker prefix not detected after max deltas; emitted buffered text.")
   289→            else:
   290→                logger.debug(f"Handled response.text.delta (buffering), total buffer length: {len(joined)}")
   291→        except Exception as e:
   292→            logger.error(f"Error in handle_text_delta: {str(e)}", exc_info=True)
   293→
   294→    async def handle_response_created(data):
   295→        # Only clear UI on the first response after an explicit Start from the user
   296→        nonlocal clear_on_next_response, response_buffer, marker_seen, delta_counter
   297→        response_buffer = []
   298→        marker_seen = False
   299→        delta_counter = 0
   300→        logger.info(f"Handled response.created, clearing buffer and resetting marker state")
   301→        if clear_on_next_response:
   302→            await websocket.send_text(json.dumps({
   303→                "type": "text",
   304→                "content": "",
   305→                "isNewResponse": True
   306→            }, ensure_ascii=False))
   307→            clear_on_next_response = False
   308→        logger.info("Handled response.created")
   309→
   310→    async def handle_error(data):
   311→        error_msg = data.get("error", {}).get("message", "Unknown error")
   312→        logger.error(f"OpenAI error: {error_msg}")
   313→        await websocket.send_text(json.dumps({
   314→            "type": "error",
   315→            "content": error_msg
   316→        }, ensure_ascii=False))
   317→        # Ensure clients exit generating state even if OpenAI aborts the turn
   318→        await websocket.send_text(json.dumps({
   319→            "type": "status",
   320→            "status": "idle"
   321→        }, ensure_ascii=False))
   322→        logger.info("Handled error message from OpenAI")
   323→
   324→    async def handle_response_done(data):
   325→        nonlocal client, response_buffer, marker_seen
   326→        logger.info(f"Handled response.done (marker_seen={marker_seen}, buffer_size={len(response_buffer)})")
   327→        if not marker_seen and response_buffer:
   328→            logger.info("Flushing remaining buffer content")
   329→            await flush_buffer()
   330→            marker_seen = True
   331→        
   332→        recording_stopped.set()
   333→        
   334→        # Update frontend status to idle
   335→        try:
   336→            await websocket.send_text(json.dumps({
   337→                "type": "status",
   338→                "status": "idle"
   339→            }, ensure_ascii=False))
   340→        except Exception as e:
   341→            logger.error(f"Error sending status after response done: {str(e)}", exc_info=True)
   342→        
   343→        # Close the OpenAI client connection (not the user WebSocket)
   344→        # The user WebSocket should remain open for the next request
   345→        if client:
   346→            logger.info("Response completed, closing OpenAI client connection")
   347→            try:
   348→                await client.close()
   349→                client = None
   350→                openai_ready.clear()
   351→            except Exception as e:
   352→                logger.error(f"Error closing OpenAI client: {str(e)}", exc_info=True)
   353→
   354→    async def handle_generic_event(event_type, data):
   355→        logger.info(f"Handled {event_type} with data: {json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False)}")
   356→
   357→    # Create a queue to handle incoming audio chunks
   358→    audio_queue = asyncio.Queue()
   359→
   360→    async def receive_messages():
   361→        nonlocal client
   362→        logger.info("receive_messages task started")
   363→        
   364→        try:
   365→            while True:
   366→                if websocket.client_state == WebSocketState.DISCONNECTED:
   367→                    logger.info("WebSocket client disconnected")
   368→                    openai_ready.clear()
   369→                    break
   370→                    
   371→                try:
   372→                    # Add timeout to prevent infinite waiting
   373→                    logger.info("Waiting for message from client (timeout=30s)...")
   374→                    data = await asyncio.wait_for(websocket.receive(), timeout=30.0)
   375→                    logger.info(f"Received data from client: {list(data.keys())}")
   376→                except asyncio.CancelledError:
   377→                    logger.info("Receive messages task cancelled")
   378→                    raise
   379→                except asyncio.TimeoutError:
   380→                    logger.debug("No message received for 30 seconds")
   381→                    continue
   382→                except Exception as e:
   383→                    logger.error(f"Error receiving message: {str(e)}", exc_info=True)
   384→                    break
   385→                
   386→                if "bytes" in data:
   387→                    processed_audio = audio_processor.process_audio_chunk(data["bytes"])
   388→                    if not openai_ready.is_set():
   389→                        logger.debug("OpenAI not ready, buffering audio chunk")
   390→                        pending_audio_chunks.append(processed_audio)
   391→                    elif client and is_recording:
   392→                        await client.send_audio(processed_audio)
   393→                        await websocket.send_text(json.dumps({
   394→                            "type": "status",
   395→                            "status": "connected"
   396→                        }, ensure_ascii=False))
   397→                        logger.debug(f"Sent audio chunk, size: {len(processed_audio)} bytes")
   398→                    else:
   399→                        logger.warning("Received audio but client is not initialized")
   400→                            
   401→                elif "text" in data:
   402→                    msg = json.loads(data["text"])
   403→                    logger.info(f"Received message from client: {msg.get('type')}")
   404→                    
   405→                    if msg.get("type") == "start_recording":
   406→                        logger.info("Processing start_recording request")
   407→                        
   408→                        # Update status to connecting while initializing realtime client
   409→                        await websocket.send_text(json.dumps({
   410→                            "type": "status",
   411→                            "status": "connecting"
   412→                        }, ensure_ascii=False))
   413→                        model = msg.get("model")
   414→                        logger.info(f"Received start_recording: model={model}")
   415→                        if not await initialize_realtime_client(model=model):
   416→                            continue
   417→                        recording_stopped.clear()
   418→                        pending_audio_chunks.clear()
   419→                        # Immediately clear transcript for a new client-initiated request
   420→                        await websocket.send_text(json.dumps({
   421→                            "type": "text",
   422→                            "content": "",
   423→                            "isNewResponse": True
   424→                        }, ensure_ascii=False))
   425→                        clear_on_next_response = False
   426→                        is_recording = True
   427→                        
   428→                        # Send any buffered chunks
   429→                        if pending_audio_chunks and client:
   430→                            logger.info(f"Sending {len(pending_audio_chunks)} buffered chunks")
   431→                            for chunk in pending_audio_chunks:
   432→                                await client.send_audio(chunk)
   433→                            pending_audio_chunks.clear()
   434→                        
   435→                    elif msg.get("type") == "stop_recording":
   436→                        # On explicit Stop, force-commit and force-create a response, then wait for completion.
   437→                        if client:
   438→                            # Immediately stop accepting further audio for this turn
   439→                            is_recording = False
   440→                            try:
   441→                                await client.commit_audio()
   442→                                logger.info("Audio committed, starting response...")
   443→                                await client.start_response(PROMPTS['paraphrase-gpt-realtime-enhanced'])
   444→                                logger.info("Response started successfully")
   445→                            except Exception as e:
   446→                                logger.error(f"Error committing/starting response on stop: {str(e)}", exc_info=True)
   447→                                # If we fail to kick off a response, surface that we're no longer recording
   448→                                await websocket.send_text(json.dumps({
   449→                                    "type": "status",
   450→                                    "status": "idle"
   451→                                }, ensure_ascii=False))
   452→                                continue
   453→                            # Wait until the response is finished
   454→                            await recording_stopped.wait()
   455→                
   456→        finally:
   457→            # Cleanup when the loop exits
   458→            if client:
   459→                try:
   460→                    await client.close()
   461→                except Exception as e:
   462→                    logger.error(f"Error closing client in receive_messages: {str(e)}")
   463→            logger.info("Receive messages loop ended")
   464→
   465→    async def send_audio_messages():
   466→        try:
   467→            while True:
   468→                
   469→                try:
   470→                    processed_audio = await audio_queue.get()
   471→                    if processed_audio is None:
   472→                        break
   473→                    
   474→                    # Add validation
   475→                    if len(processed_audio) == 0:
   476→                        logger.warning("Empty audio chunk received, skipping")
   477→                        continue
   478→                    
   479→                    # Append the processed audio to the buffer
   480→                    audio_buffer.append(processed_audio)
   481→
   482→                    await client.send_audio(processed_audio)
   483→                    logger.info(f"Audio chunk sent to OpenAI client, size: {len(processed_audio)} bytes")
   484→                    
   485→                except asyncio.CancelledError:
   486→                    logger.info("Send audio messages task cancelled")
   487→                    raise
   488→                except Exception as e:
   489→                    logger.error(f"Error in send_audio_messages: {str(e)}", exc_info=True)
   490→                    break
   491→        except asyncio.CancelledError:
   492→            logger.info("Send audio messages task cancelled")
   493→            raise
   494→
   495→        # After processing all audio, set the event
   496→        recording_stopped.set()
   497→
   498→    # Start concurrent tasks for receiving and sending
   499→    receive_task = asyncio.create_task(receive_messages())
   500→    send_task = asyncio.create_task(send_audio_messages())
   501→
   502→    try:
   503→        # Wait for both tasks to complete
   504→        await asyncio.gather(receive_task, send_task)
   505→    except Exception as e:
   506→        logger.error(f"Error in WebSocket connection: {e}", exc_info=True)
   507→    finally:
   508→        # Cancel background tasks before cleanup
   509→        receive_task.cancel()
   510→        send_task.cancel()
   511→        
   512→        # Wait for tasks to be cancelled (with timeout)
   513→        try:
   514→            await asyncio.wait_for(asyncio.gather(receive_task, send_task, return_exceptions=True), timeout=1.0)
   515→        except asyncio.TimeoutError:
   516→            logger.warning("Tasks did not cancel within timeout")
   517→        except Exception as e:
   518→            logger.debug(f"Error cancelling tasks: {e}")
   519→        
   520→        if client:
   521→            await client.close()
   522→        if websocket.client_state != WebSocketState.DISCONNECTED:
   523→            try:
   524→                await websocket.close()
   525→            except RuntimeError as e:
   526→                logger.warning(f"Ignoring error during websocket close: {e}")
   527→        logger.info("WebSocket connection closed for /api/v1/ws")
   528→
   529→@app.post(
   530→    "/api/v1/readability",
   531→    response_model=ReadabilityResponse,
   532→    summary="Enhance Text Readability",
   533→    description="Improve the readability of the provided text using GPT-4o."
   534→)
   535→async def enhance_readability(request: ReadabilityRequest):
   536→    prompt = PROMPTS.get('readability-enhance')
   537→    if not prompt:
   538→        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail="Readability prompt not found.")
   539→
   540→    try:
   541→        async def text_generator():
   542→            # Use gpt-4o specifically for readability
   543→            async for part in llm_processor.process_text(request.text, prompt, model="gpt-4o"):
   544→                yield part
   545→
   546→        return StreamingResponse(text_generator(), media_type="text/plain")
   547→
   548→    except Exception as e:
   549→        logger.error(f"Error enhancing readability: {e}", exc_info=True)
   550→        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail="Error processing readability enhancement.")
   551→

   571→
   572→@app.post(
   573→    "/api/v1/correctness",
   574→    response_model=CorrectnessResponse,
   575→    summary="Check Factual Correctness",
   576→    description="Analyze the text for factual accuracy using GPT-4o."
   577→)
   578→async def check_correctness(request: CorrectnessRequest):
   579→    prompt = PROMPTS.get('correctness-check')
   580→    if not prompt:
   581→        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail="Correctness prompt not found.")
   582→
   583→    try:
   584→        async def text_generator():

   588→        return StreamingResponse(text_generator(), media_type="text/plain")
   589→
   590→    except Exception as e:
   591→        logger.error(f"Error checking correctness: {e}", exc_info=True)
   592→        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail="Error processing correctness check.")

   193→/* ===== CARD STACK ===== */
   194→
   195→/** Build a single card element and append it to cs-cards. */
   196→function buildCard({ type, label, text, meta }) {
   197→    const words    = text.trim().split(/\s+/);
   198→    const preview  = words.slice(0, 15).join(' ') + (words.length > 15 ? '…' : '');
   199→
   200→    const card = document.createElement('div');
   201→    card.className = 'cs-card';
   202→    card.dataset.type = type;
   203→    card.setAttribute('role', 'listitem');
   204→
   205→    card.innerHTML = `
   206→      <div class="cs-card-header" role="button" tabindex="0"
   207→           aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="cs-body-${type}">
   208→        <span class="cs-card-label">${escapeHtml(label)}</span>
   209→        <span class="cs-card-preview">${escapeHtml(preview)}</span>
   210→        <span class="cs-card-chevron" aria-hidden="true">
   211→          <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor"
   212→               stroke-width="2.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round">
   213→            <polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9"/>
   214→          </svg>
   215→        </span>
   216→      </div>
   217→      <div class="cs-card-body" id="cs-body-${type}" role="region">
   218→        <div class="cs-card-body-inner">
   219→          <div class="cs-card-meta">
   220→            ${meta.map(m => `<span>${escapeHtml(m)}</span>`).join('')}
   221→          </div>
   222→          <div class="cs-card-text">${escapeHtml(text)}</div>
   223→          <div class="cs-card-actions">
   224→            <button class="cs-action-btn" data-action="readability" disabled>Readability</button>
   225→            <button class="cs-action-btn" data-action="inspire" disabled>Inspire</button>
   226→            <button class="cs-action-btn" data-action="copy" disabled>Copy</button>
   227→            <button class="cs-action-btn" data-action="send" disabled>Send</button>
   228→          </div>
   229→        </div>
   230→      </div>
   231→    `;
   232→
   233→    const header = card.querySelector('.cs-card-header');
   234→
   235→    function toggleCard() {
   236→        const isExpanded = card.classList.contains('cs-expanded');
   237→        // Collapse all other cards (accordion)
   238→        csCards.querySelectorAll('.cs-card.cs-expanded').forEach(c => {
   239→            if (c !== card) {
   240→                c.classList.remove('cs-expanded');
   241→                c.querySelector('.cs-card-header').setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
   242→            }
   243→        });
   244→        card.classList.toggle('cs-expanded', !isExpanded);
   245→        header.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(!isExpanded));
   246→    }
   247→
   248→    header.addEventListener('click', toggleCard);
   249→    header.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
   250→        if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') { e.preventDefault(); toggleCard(); }
   251→    });
   252→
   253→    return card;
   254→}
   255→
   256→/** Formats milliseconds as "m:ss" duration string. */
   257→function formatDuration(ms) {
   258→    const totalSecs = Math.round(ms / 1000);
   259→    const mins = Math.floor(totalSecs / 60);
   260→    const secs = totalSecs % 60;
   261→    return `${mins}:${String(secs).padStart(2, '0')}`;
   262→}
   263→
   264→/** Show the Card Stack view with the given brainstorm transcript. */
   265→function showCardStack(text, durationMs) {
   266→    // Clear previous cards
   267→    csCards.innerHTML = '';
   268→
   269→    const wordCount = text.trim().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).length;
   270→    const durationStr = durationMs > 0 ? formatDuration(durationMs) : null;
   271→    const meta = [
   272→        durationStr ? `${durationStr}` : null,
   273→        `${wordCount} word${wordCount !== 1 ? 's' : ''}`,
   274→    ].filter(Boolean);
   275→
   276→    const card = buildCard({ type: 'transcript', label: 'Transcript', text, meta });
   277→    csCards.appendChild(card);
   278→
   279→    // Start expanded since it's the only card
   280→    card.classList.add('cs-expanded');
   281→    card.querySelector('.cs-card-header').setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
   282→
   283→    // Slide in
   284→    cardStackView.hidden = false;
   285→    // Force a reflow so the transition fires
   286→    void cardStackView.offsetWidth;
   287→    cardStackView.classList.add('cs-visible');
   288→}
   289→
   290→/** Hide the Card Stack view and return to main screen. */
   291→function hideCardStack() {
   292→    cardStackView.classList.remove('cs-visible');
   293→    // Wait for the slide-out animation, then hide
   294→    cardStackView.addEventListener('transitionend', function onEnd() {
   295→        cardStackView.removeEventListener('transitionend', onEnd);
   296→        cardStackView.hidden = true;
   297→        csCards.innerHTML = '';
   298→    }, { once: true });
   299→}
   300→
   301→/** Immediately hide the card stack without animation (used when recording restarts). */
   302→function hideCardStackInstant() {
   303→    cardStackView.classList.remove('cs-visible');
   304→    cardStackView.hidden = true;
   305→    csCards.innerHTML = '';
   306→}
   307→
   308→csBackBtn.addEventListener('click', hideCardStack);
   309→
   310→/* ===== MESSAGE HISTORY ===== */
   311→function addMessage(text, timestamp, status) {
   312→    messagesEmpty.style.display = 'none';
   313→    const el = document.createElement('div');
   314→    el.className = `message ${status}`;
   315→    const timeStr = new Date(timestamp).toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });
   316→    const statusLabel = { sending: 'Sending…', sent: 'Sent', error: 'Failed', 'no-webhook': 'No webhook' };
   317→    el.innerHTML = `
   318→        <p class="message-text">${escapeHtml(text)}</p>
   319→        <div class="message-meta">
   320→            <span class="message-time">${timeStr}</span>
   321→            <span class="message-status">${statusLabel[status] || status}</span>
   322→        </div>
   323→    `;
   324→    messagesEl.appendChild(el);
   325→    el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'end' });
   326→    return el;
   327→}
   328→
   329→function updateMessageStatus(el, status) {
   330→    el.className = `message ${status}`;
   331→    const statusEl = el.querySelector('.message-status');
   332→    const labels = { sent: 'Sent', error: 'Failed', 'no-webhook': 'No webhook set' };
   333→    if (statusEl) statusEl.textContent = labels[status] || status;
   334→}
   335→
   336→/* ===== WEBHOOK SEND ===== */
   337→async function sendMessage(text, source) {
   338→    const webhookUrl = localStorage.getItem('webhookUrl') || '';
   339→    const timestamp  = new Date().toISOString();
   340→    const payload    = { text, source, timestamp };
   341→    const msgEl      = addMessage(text, timestamp, 'sending');
   342→
   343→    if (!webhookUrl) {
   344→        updateMessageStatus(msgEl, 'no-webhook');
   345→        return;
   346→    }
   347→
   348→    try {
   349→        const res = await fetch(webhookUrl, {
   350→            method:  'POST',
   351→            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
   352→            body:    JSON.stringify(payload),
   353→        });
   354→        if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);
   355→        updateMessageStatus(msgEl, 'sent');
   356→    } catch (err) {
   357→        console.warn('Webhook error:', err);
   358→        updateMessageStatus(msgEl, 'error');
   359→    }
   360→}
   361→
   362→/* ===== TRANSCRIPT BAR ACTIONS ===== */
   363→sendBtn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
   364→    const text   = transcriptText.textContent.trim();
   365→    const source = transcriptBar.dataset.source || 'quick';
   366→    if (!text) return;
   367→    hideTranscriptBar();
   368→    await sendMessage(text, source);
   369→});
   370→
   371→discardBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
   372→    hideTranscriptBar();
   373→});
   374→
   375→/* ===== BRAINSTORM POST-PROCESSING ===== */
   376→
   377→function getBackendBaseUrl() {
   378→    const backendUrl = localStorage.getItem('brainwaveBackendUrl') || '';
   379→    if (backendUrl) {
   380→        try {
   381→            const url = new URL(backendUrl);
   382→            return `${url.protocol}//${url.host}`;
   383→        } catch (_) { /* fall through to default */ }
   384→    }
   385→    return `${window.location.protocol}//${window.location.host}`;
   386→}
   387→
   388→let _postprocessedContent = '';
   389→
   390→async function runPostProcess(endpoint, label, activeBtn) {
   391→    const text = transcriptText.textContent.trim();
   392→    if (!text) return;
   393→
   394→    // Reset result area
   395→    _postprocessedContent = '';
   396→    postprocessText.textContent = '';
   397→    postprocessLabel.textContent = label;
   398→    postprocessResultActions.hidden = true;
   399→
   400→    // Disable both buttons; show loading on the active one
   401→    readabilityBtn.disabled = true;
   402→    inspireBtn.disabled = true;
   403→    const origLabel = activeBtn.textContent;
   404→    activeBtn.textContent = 'Processing…';
   405→
   406→    // Show the result area (empty initially so user sees it appeared)
   407→    postprocessResult.hidden = false;
   408→
   409→    try {
   410→        const response = await fetch(`${getBackendBaseUrl()}${endpoint}`, {
   411→            method:  'POST',
   412→            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
   413→            body:    JSON.stringify({ text }),
   414→        });
   415→
   416→        if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${response.status}`);
   417→
   418→        const reader  = response.body.getReader();
   419→        const decoder = new TextDecoder();
   420→
   421→        while (true) {
   422→            const { done, value } = await reader.read();
   423→            if (done) break;
   424→            _postprocessedContent += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });
   425→            postprocessText.textContent = _postprocessedContent;
   426→        }
   427→
   428→        // Streaming complete — show Send button
   429→        postprocessResultActions.hidden = false;
   430→
   431→    } catch (err) {
   432→        console.error('Post-processing error:', err);
   433→        postprocessText.textContent = 'Error: could not process. Check connection and try again.';
   434→    } finally {
   435→        activeBtn.textContent = origLabel;
   436→        readabilityBtn.disabled = false;
   437→        inspireBtn.disabled = false;
   438→    }
   439→}
   440→
   441→readabilityBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
   442→    runPostProcess('/api/v1/readability', 'Readability', readabilityBtn);
   443→});
   444→
   445→inspireBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
   446→    runPostProcess('/api/v1/correctness', 'Inspire', inspireBtn);
   447→});
   448→
   449→postprocessSendBtn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
   450→    const text = _postprocessedContent.trim();
   451→    if (!text) return;
   452→    hideTranscriptBar();
   453→    await sendMessage(text, 'brainstorm');
   454→});
   455→
   456→/* ===== SETTINGS MODAL ===== */
   457→settingsBtn.addEventListener('click', openSettings);
   458→modalClose.addEventListener('click', closeSettings);
   459→modalOverlay.addEventListener('click', (e) => { if (e.target === modalOverlay) closeSettings(); });
   460→document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => { if (e.key === 'Escape') closeSettings(); });
   461→
   462→saveSettings.addEventListener('click', () => {
   463→    const url = webhookInput.value.trim();
   464→    localStorage.setItem('webhookUrl', url);
   465→
   466→    const backendUrl = backendUrlInput.value.trim();
   467→    if (backendUrl) {
   468→        localStorage.setItem('brainwaveBackendUrl', backendUrl);
   469→    } else {
   470→        localStorage.removeItem('brainwaveBackendUrl');
   471→    }
   472→
   473→    const lang     = langSelect.value;
   474→    const prevLang = localStorage.getItem('recognitionLang') || '';
   475→    if (lang !== prevLang) {
   476→        if (lang) {
   477→            localStorage.setItem('recognitionLang', lang);
   478→        } else {
   479→            localStorage.removeItem('recognitionLang');
   480→        }
   481→        // Re-init recognition so new language takes effect immediately
   482→        if (!isListening) initRecognition();
   483→        // Re-init wake recognition for new language too
   484→        if (!wakeWordListening) initWakeRecognition();
